Director of the hospital at ul. The water main tried to save the situation by taking all possible measures to supplement the vacancies. Unfortunately, even the prospect of attractive earnings did not convince us to work in a hospital, including doctors from outside the region. Finally, I would like to stress that the psychiatry department can be suspended at any time. If the funds are guaranteed and doctors are willing to work, the branch can resume its activity on an old basis at any time. The problem is not only about our voivodship, it is a nationwide problem. He experiences it, for example. also Dolnośląskie voivodship. And that's where the hospital directors are forced to close or suspend psychiatric wards. For months, hospital representatives have reported their problems to the Ministry. Unfortunately, despite many appeals, the matter remains unresolved and growing. Therefore, once again, I would like to call on the Minister of Health to present as soon as possible solutions that will remedy the current situation in terms of lack of staff and financial resources for psychiatry. It should also be stressed that local authorities, within their limited financial capacity, can only correct certain shortcomings of the system - real tools and resources have a government in this regard. ParlaMint-PL_2021-09-29-sejm-38-1.u611	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! Last week, September 21st, we celebrated the World Day of Alzheimer's Disease. This day has been celebrated since 1994. The purpose of establishing such a day is to raise public awareness and combat stigmatisation of disease and disease. It is in September that Alzheimer's organisations around the world organize various educational and information events, marches, media campaigns and fundraisers for their activities. While such campaigns are usually successful because they increase awareness of the disease, stigmatisation, lack of reliable information, lack of support services for the sick and their loved ones are a global problem in the fight against this disease. This disease is one of the most serious diseases associated with civilization, accelerated pace and technosis of life. It should also be noted that progress in various areas of medical science, as well as improving living conditions, increases the average length of human life, which increases the likelihood of dementia, especially Alzheimer's disease. The problem is serious and constantly growing. In Poland statistics show that about 500,000 people currently suffer from Alzheimer's disease and other dementias. by 2050, the number of patients will rise to 1.3 million. Alzheimer's disease involves all family members. The caretaker of the sick person is most often the spouse, including the elderly. Many years of long-term, 24-hour care is a huge psychological and physical burden, it can be so exhausting that it leads the caretaker to a complete loss of strength and health. An important support for these families is the form of specialized daily residence homes where the patient remains under the supervision of properly prepared staff. At present, such centres are few, and their coverage does not solve this problem to a large extent. That is why I believe that this is where we should be able to agree so that we can create a number of solutions together in order to help people who are ill and in large part their families. Supervision of an Alzheimer's person must be ensured for 24 hours. In a large number of cases, families cannot fulfill this obligation, which translates into the various dangers that the sick face. Many European countries have seen this problem and taken appropriate action. In 2009, the Council The European Parliament has adopted a declaration on making Alzheimer's disease a priority for health policy in the countries of the European Union, recommending the development of national Alzheimer's plans. Such plans are already being implemented in many countries in the Community. They allow proper organisation and funding of care for this particular group of patients. The assumptions of the Alzheimer's plan for Poland were developed by the Alzheimer's Coalition, which is created by the Polish Alzheimer's Organization Agreement and the Polish Alzheimer's Society. The assumptions of the national Alzheimer's plan for Poland were passed on to the Ministry of Health in 2011. The development and implementation of the Polish Alzheimer's plan by the government is becoming the most important challenge in order to define a comprehensive strategy of action in the fight against Alzheimer's disease, taking into account the particular situation and role of families of patients. That is why I call once again on politicians of all groups and governments to fight this disease together so that we can ensure the safety and care of the sick and their families. Next year, I would like Poland to be shown as a country which is an example in Europe and the world in providing comprehensive treatment and care for people suffering from Alzheimer's disease. It is impossible not to mention the sick themselves and their families here. I wish you perseverance in fighting the adversity of fate and the constant fight for health. The best wish for you should nevertheless be a wish to succeed in overcoming this disease.
ParlaMint-PL_2021-09-29-sejm-38-1.u612	History of history in Piotrków Trybunalski The beginning of history education still within the Faculty of the University of Pedagogical Education in Kielce is located in 1997, when the then Ministry of National Education granted permission for a five-year Master's degree in history, thus appreciating the forming academic community in the Piotrków region. From the very beginning of the existence of a direction within a separate institute, the authorities were two-way caring for its development and quality of education, successfully gathering staff, graduates of leading universities of the country (Warsaw University, Jagiellonian University in Cracow, University of Cracow). Adam Mickiewicz in Poznań, University of Łódź), and they were able to integrate representatives of the region around the institute. An important contribution to the history of the region is the number of students, scholarships of the Minister of Education and Science. For several years, a representative of history has received a scholarship every year to become actively involved in activities related to social responsibility of science (organization of conferences, volunteering within the Piotrków University of the First Age or educational activity at the Marzipan Museum). Scientific conferences, including many cyclical ones, are also an important element of history's activity. They are organized both by the strict environment Scientific (e.g. The National Scientific Conference of the Baltics in the Byzantine perspective in the 1000th anniversary of the Battle of Kleidion, the National Scientific Conference of the Polish Wars in the last year of World War II, the 4th National Scientific Conference of the Left. PPS and legions, the popular science conference, 100 years of State Police, Historical Workshops to the end of the faithful... Martyrology of the State Police in the East, the National Scientific Conference of the Church's role in preserving the integrity of Polish lands), as well as students (18 views of the National Student-Doctoran Scientific Conference of the People and History, four views of the National Student-Doctoran Scientific Conference of the Women throughout history). Nor should we forget the role of popularization and strengthening historical knowledge. An important element of this action has been the 1998 periodical of the Piotrków Historical Books and the IHiSM scientific publication on the ministerial list, harmoniously combining the publications of researchers from Poland and abroad, the debuts of students in scientific assistance and within the framework of the social responsibility of science, helping to publish history enthusiasts without appropriate education (the latter initiative is particularly important at the present time when the availability of digital instruments allows the spread of unverified knowledge, often erroneous and perverting historical truth; publications in the periodical and publishing are not only properly reviewed, but also their authors are offered substantive care). There are also initiatives related to the social responsibility of science implemented jointly with governmental and local authorities such as: Piotrkowski University of the First Age, Marzipan Museum, Academic Night of Museums, Tribunal meetings with history, Historical Family Festival, Charity Court. Since December 2020, the museum has been able to visit the halls dedicated to the history of the Polish Legions by Józef Piłsudski and their relationships with Piotrków Trybunalski (financed by the support of the Marshal's Office in Łódź). This permanent exhibition is an extremely valuable addition to the educational offer aimed not only at students but also at secondary school students and primary school pupils. Knowledge of the richness and importance of the history of the region is simply unrecognizable as a contribution to patriotic education, and its transmission is an irreparable element of history-oriented education. It is also worth noting that not only academic teachers but also students of the direction are involved in the popularization of this knowledge. In 2018, at the request of the city authorities in connection with the 800th anniversary of Piotrków, lectures took the form of talks during walking through the streets of the city to return to the classic form of Grodzki meetings with history in the next year. Since 2020, however, these meetings have been organised online or stationary at the new museum headquarters. These initiatives are complemented by organisation and regular lectures (e.g. Ricky and its effects, Katyn's Crime - the Way to Truth, the Christmas of the Piotrkowians in the 19th century, the Saints and the Saints, and the Monasteries of Piotrków once again). The above events and projects, co-organized by the Association of Piotrków Academic Brotherhood im. Zygmunt and the Old enrich and strengthen the declining historical knowledge among the youngest. These shortcomings, which should be regretted, in turn, translate into a low level of civic and patriotic awareness. An important element of these initiatives is also the fact that they show how attractive it is to spend your free time, deepening your interests and passions. Pro-social actions are also effectively complemented by strict historical initiatives promoting knowledge not only about events from universal or national history, but also about such valuable and sometimes underestimated knowledge about the history of a small homeland. All these initiatives promoting knowledge, patriotic attitudes, pro-Citizen and pro-state actions will be destroyed along with the eradication of the direction and universities, and the resulting educational rift will not patch up any cyclical and temporary action. The direction of history, we remind you, has existed since 1997 and can therefore exploit its considerable potential and experience in working for the region and the country.
